# Normalized direct-image comparison helpers

## Reason

The assigned tensor/pullback proof needs the actual normalized direct-image natural
isomorphism in a public construction of its underived mate. Its current private
visibility prevents that use. Expose the existing isomorphism and its two helper
declarations under the same names, with their types and bodies unchanged. The module
Sheaf.Paper.FiniteDiscreteEncodingPullbackFixed was unclaimed when checked.

## Old

```lean
private theorem sectionPushforward_smul (T U : Scheme.{u}) (f : U ⟶ T)
    (eT : Encoding A T) (eU : Encoding A U) (M : Internal A U)
    (V : T.ProEtᵒᵖ) (a : ULift.{u + 1} A)
    (m : M.X.obj.obj ((MorphismProperty.Over.pullback @WeaklyEtale ⊤ f).op.obj V)) :
    (show M.X.obj.obj ((MorphismProperty.Over.pullback @WeaklyEtale ⊤ f).op.obj V) from
      (ModuleCat.smul (((sectionEncoding A T eT.ringIso ⋙ fixedRestriction A T).obj
      ((CondensedRing.lowerStar (coefficient A) f).obj M)).obj.obj V) a).hom m) =
    (show M.X.obj.obj ((MorphismProperty.Over.pullback @WeaklyEtale ⊤ f).op.obj V) from
      (ModuleCat.smul (((lowerStar (ModuleCat.{u + 1} (ULift.{u + 1} A)) f).obj
      ((sectionEncoding A U eU.ringIso ⋙ fixedRestriction A U).obj M)).obj.obj V) a).hom m)
```

```lean
private def sectionPushforwardIso (T U : Scheme.{u}) (f : U ⟶ T)
    (eT : Encoding A T) (eU : Encoding A U) (M : Internal A U) :
    (sectionEncoding A T eT.ringIso ⋙ fixedRestriction A T).obj
      ((CondensedRing.lowerStar (coefficient A) f).obj M) ≅
    (lowerStar (ModuleCat.{u + 1} (ULift.{u + 1} A)) f).obj
      ((sectionEncoding A U eU.ringIso ⋙ fixedRestriction A U).obj M)
```

```lean
private def encodingPushforwardNatIso (T U : Scheme.{u}) (f : U ⟶ T)
    (eT : Encoding A T) (eU : Encoding A U) :
    CondensedRing.lowerStar (coefficient A) f ⋙ eT.ordinary.functor ⋙ eT.fixed.functor ≅
      (eU.ordinary.functor ⋙ eU.fixed.functor) ⋙
        lowerStar (ModuleCat.{u + 1} (ULift.{u + 1} A)) f
```

## New

```lean
theorem sectionPushforward_smul (T U : Scheme.{u}) (f : U ⟶ T)
    (eT : Encoding A T) (eU : Encoding A U) (M : Internal A U)
    (V : T.ProEtᵒᵖ) (a : ULift.{u + 1} A)
    (m : M.X.obj.obj ((MorphismProperty.Over.pullback @WeaklyEtale ⊤ f).op.obj V)) :
    (show M.X.obj.obj ((MorphismProperty.Over.pullback @WeaklyEtale ⊤ f).op.obj V) from
      (ModuleCat.smul (((sectionEncoding A T eT.ringIso ⋙ fixedRestriction A T).obj
      ((CondensedRing.lowerStar (coefficient A) f).obj M)).obj.obj V) a).hom m) =
    (show M.X.obj.obj ((MorphismProperty.Over.pullback @WeaklyEtale ⊤ f).op.obj V) from
      (ModuleCat.smul (((lowerStar (ModuleCat.{u + 1} (ULift.{u + 1} A)) f).obj
      ((sectionEncoding A U eU.ringIso ⋙ fixedRestriction A U).obj M)).obj.obj V) a).hom m)
```

```lean
def sectionPushforwardIso (T U : Scheme.{u}) (f : U ⟶ T)
    (eT : Encoding A T) (eU : Encoding A U) (M : Internal A U) :
    (sectionEncoding A T eT.ringIso ⋙ fixedRestriction A T).obj
      ((CondensedRing.lowerStar (coefficient A) f).obj M) ≅
    (lowerStar (ModuleCat.{u + 1} (ULift.{u + 1} A)) f).obj
      ((sectionEncoding A U eU.ringIso ⋙ fixedRestriction A U).obj M)
```

```lean
def encodingPushforwardNatIso (T U : Scheme.{u}) (f : U ⟶ T)
    (eT : Encoding A T) (eU : Encoding A U) :
    CondensedRing.lowerStar (coefficient A) f ⋙ eT.ordinary.functor ⋙ eT.fixed.functor ≅
      (eU.ordinary.functor ⋙ eU.fixed.functor) ⋙
        lowerStar (ModuleCat.{u + 1} (ULift.{u + 1} A)) f
```

No construction, scalar action, theorem conclusion, public import, or target
meaning changes. Existing consumers using `import all` still refer to these same
declarations. No consumer adaptation is required. The matching node adds these
existing declarations to its Lean attachment.
